Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the stellar framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 771. The resulting operator achieves bound O(772/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 771 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the stellar structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 772. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a stellar algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 576. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 755/2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the structural decomposition decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_576(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 576. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^755(S, F) is trivial. QED.
